    """Data fields for the COBOL program."""
    WS_FOUND: bool = False
    LOAN_DELINQUENT: bool = False
    WS_LATE_PAYMENT_FEE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TOTAL_FEES: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_NOT_EOF: bool = False
    WS_EOF: bool = False
    INS_LIFE: bool = False
    INS_HEALTH: bool = False
    INS_AUTO: bool = False
    INS_HOME: bool = False
    INS_UMBRELLA: bool = False
    INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_LIFE_RATE_PER_1000: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_HEALTH_BASE_PREMIUM: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_AUTO_BASE_PREMIUM: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_HOME_RATE_PER_1000: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_UMBRELLA_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    INS_CLAIMS_COUNT: int = 0
    WS_CALC_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    INS_PREMIUM_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    INV_MARKET_VALUE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    INV_QUANTITY: int = 0
    INV_CURRENT_PRICE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    INV_PURCHASE_PRICE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    INV_GAIN_LOSS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    INV_DIVIDEND_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TOTAL_DIVIDENDS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_CURRENT_DATE: str = ""
    REPORT_LINE: str = ""
    WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT: str = ""
    WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TOTAL_LOANS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TEMP_DATE: str = ""
    WS_FORMATTED_DATE: str = ""
    ACCT_ID: str = ""
    WS_VALID: bool = False
    WS_INVALID: bool = False
    WS_BRACKET_1_MAX: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_BRACKET_1_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_BRACKET_2_MAX: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_BRACKET_2_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_BRACKET_3_MAX: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_BRACKET_3_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_BRACKET_5_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_CALC_TAX: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_CUST_COUNT: int = 0
    WS_ACCT_COUNT: int = 0
    WS_TRAN_COUNT: int = 0
    WS_LOAN_COUNT: int = 0
    WS_ERROR_COUNT: int = 0
    WS_FORMATTED_COUNT: str = ""
    WS_TOTAL_INTEREST: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_CURRENT_TIMESTAMP: str = ""
    TRAN_TIMESTAMP: str = ""
    TRAN_TYPE: str = ""
    TRAN_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    TRAN_STATUS: str = ""
    AUD_TIMESTAMP: str = ""
    WS_PROCESS_COUNT: int = 0
    CUST_CREDIT_SCORE: int = 0
    CUST_TOTAL_LOANS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_CALC_RESULT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    CUST_RISK_RATING: str = ""
    ACCT_OVERDRAFT_LIMIT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_NOT_APPROVED: bool = False
    WS_APPROVED: bool = False
    ACCT_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_CREDIT_CARD_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_CALC_INTEREST: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    LOAN_PAYMENT_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    LOAN_LTV_RATIO: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    LOAN_CURRENT_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    LOAN_COLLATERAL_VALUE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_LOAN_ORIGINATION_PCT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_CALC_FEE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    INV_STOCKS: bool = False
    INV_BONDS: bool = False
    INV_MUTUAL_FUND: bool = False
    WS_TEMP_FLAG: str = ""
    

def conditional_set_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """ELSE
        SET ws_found TO TRUE
    
    """
    data.WS_FOUND = True

def mark_delinquent_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """3420-mark_delinquent
        SET loan_delinquent TO TRUE
    """
    data.LOAN_DELINQUENT = True

def assess_late_fee_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """3430-assess_late_fee
        ADD ws_late_payment_fee TO ws_total_fees
    """
    data.WS_TOTAL_FEES += data.WS_LATE_PAYMENT_FEE

def process_collections_C2() -> None:
    """3500-process_collections
        DISPLAY "PROCESSING COLLECTIONS..."
        CONTINUE
    """
    print("PROCESSING COLLECTIONS...")

def handle_defaults_C2() -> None:
    """3600-handle_defaults
        DISPLAY "HANDLING DEFAULTS..."
        CONTINUE
    """
    print("HANDLING DEFAULTS...")

def process_insurance_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    process_policies_C2()
    calculate_premiums_C2(data)
    process_claims_C2()
    assess_risk_C2()
    renew_policies_C2()

def process_policies_C2() -> None:
    """4100-process_policies
        DISPLAY "PROCESSING INSURANCE POLICIES..."
        CONTINUE
    """
    print("PROCESSING INSURANCE POLICIES...")

def calculate_premiums_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """4200-calculate_premiums
        DISPLAY "CALCULATING PREMIUMS..."
        SET ws_not_eof TO TRUE
        PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof
            READ insurance_master NEXT
                AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
                NOT AT END
                    PERFORM 4210-determine_base_premium
                    PERFORM 4220-apply_risk_factor
                    PERFORM 4230-calculate_final_premium
            
        
    """
    print("CALCULATING PREMIUMS...")
    data.WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not data.WS_EOF:
        # Simulate READ insurance_master NEXT
        # In a real scenario, this would involve reading from a file/database
        # For now, let's assume some dummy data and logic for demonstration'
        insurance_master_record = {  # Replace with actual record structure
            "ins_life": data.INS_LIFE,
            "ins_health": data.INS_HEALTH,
            "ins_auto": data.INS_AUTO,
            "ins_home": data.INS_HOME,
            "ins_umbrella": data.INS_UMBRELLA
        }  

        if insurance_master_record is None: # Simulate AT END
            data.WS_EOF = True
        else: # Simulate NOT AT END
            determine_base_premium_C2(data)
            apply_risk_factor_C2(data)
            calculate_final_premium_C2(data)

def determine_base_premium_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    match True:
        case data.INS_LIFE:
            data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = data.INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT / 1000 * data.WS_LIFE_RATE_PER_1000
        case data.INS_HEALTH:
            data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = data.WS_HEALTH_BASE_PREMIUM
        case data.INS_AUTO:
            data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = data.WS_AUTO_BASE_PREMIUM
        case data.INS_HOME:
            data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = data.INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT / 1000 * data.WS_HOME_RATE_PER_1000
        case data.INS_UMBRELLA:
            data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = data.WS_UMBRELLA_RATE

def apply_risk_factor_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    if data.INS_CLAIMS_COUNT > 2:
        data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT * Decimal("1.25")

def calculate_final_premium_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    data.INS_PREMIUM_AMOUNT = data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    data.WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS += data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def process_claims_C2() -> None:
    """4300-process_claims
        DISPLAY "PROCESSING INSURANCE CLAIMS..."
        CONTINUE
    """
    print("PROCESSING INSURANCE CLAIMS...")

def assess_risk_C2() -> None:
    """4400-assess_risk
        DISPLAY "ASSESSING INSURANCE RISK..."
        CONTINUE
    """
    print("ASSESSING INSURANCE RISK...")

    print("RENEWING POLICIES...")

def process_investments_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    update_market_prices_C2()
    calculate_portfolio_value_C2(data)
    process_trades_C2()
    calculate_dividends_C2(data)
    generate_tax_documents_C2()

def update_market_prices_C2() -> None:
    """5100-update_market_prices
        DISPLAY "UPDATING MARKET PRICES..."
        CONTINUE
    """
    print("UPDATING MARKET PRICES...")

def calculate_portfolio_value_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """5200-calculate_portfolio_value
        DISPLAY "CALCULATING PORTFOLIO VALUES..."
        SET ws_not_eof TO TRUE
        PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof
            READ investment_master NEXT
                AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
                NOT AT END
                    PERFORM 5210-calculate_position_value
                    PERFORM 5220-calculate_gain_loss
                    PERFORM 5230-update_totals
            
        
    """
    print("CALCULATING PORTFOLIO VALUES...")
    data.WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not data.WS_EOF:
        # Simulate READ investment_master NEXT
        # In a real scenario, this would involve reading from a file/database
        # For now, let's assume some dummy data and logic for demonstration'
        investment_master_record = {  # Replace with actual record structure
            "inv_quantity": data.INV_QUANTITY,
            "inv_current_price": data.INV_CURRENT_PRICE,
            "inv_purchase_price": data.INV_PURCHASE_PRICE
        }
        
        if investment_master_record is None: # Simulate AT END
            data.WS_EOF = True
        else: # Simulate NOT AT END
            calculate_position_value_C2(data)
            calculate_gain_loss_C2(data)
            update_totals_C2(data)

def calculate_position_value_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    data.INV_MARKET_VALUE = data.INV_QUANTITY * data.INV_CURRENT_PRICE

def calculate_gain_loss_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    data.INV_GAIN_LOSS = data.INV_MARKET_VALUE - (data.INV_QUANTITY * data.INV_PURCHASE_PRICE)

def update_totals_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """5230-update_totals
        ADD inv_market_value TO ws_total_investments
    """
    data.WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += data.INV_MARKET_VALUE

def process_trades_C2() -> None:
    """5300-process_trades
        DISPLAY "PROCESSING TRADES..."
        PERFORM 5310-process_buy_orders
        PERFORM 5320-process_sell_orders
        PERFORM 5330-settle_trades
    """
    print("PROCESSING TRADES...")
    process_buy_orders_C2()
    process_sell_orders_C2()
    settle_trades_C2()

    """5330-settle_trades

def calculate_dividends_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """5400-calculate_dividends
        DISPLAY "CALCULATING DIVIDENDS..."
        SET ws_not_eof TO TRUE
        PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof
            READ investment_master NEXT
                AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
                NOT AT END
                    IF inv_dividend_rate > 0
                        PERFORM 5410-compute_dividend
                        PERFORM 5420-post_dividend
                    
            
        
    """
    print("CALCULATING DIVIDENDS...")
    data.WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not data.WS_EOF:
        # Simulate READ investment_master NEXT
        # In a real scenario, this would involve reading from a file/database
        # For now, let's assume some dummy data and logic for demonstration'
        investment_master_record = {  # Replace with actual record structure
            "inv_dividend_rate": data.INV_DIVIDEND_RATE,
        }

        if investment_master_record is None:  # Simulate AT END
            data.WS_EOF = True
        else:  # Simulate NOT AT END
            if data.INV_DIVIDEND_RATE > 0:
                compute_dividend_C2(data)
                post_dividend_C2(data)

def compute_dividend_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = data.INV_MARKET_VALUE * data.INV_DIVIDEND_RATE / 4

def post_dividend_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """5420-post_dividend
        ADD ws_calc_amount TO ws_total_dividends
    """
    data.WS_TOTAL_DIVIDENDS += data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def generate_reports_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    daily_summary_C2(data)
    account_statements_C2()
    loan_reports_C2()
    insurance_reports_C2()
    investment_reports_C2()
    regulatory_reports_C2()
    management_reports_C2()

def daily_summary_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """6100-daily_summary
        DISPLAY "GENERATING DAILY SUMMARY..."
        MOVE SPACES TO report_line
        STRING "mega_enterprise DAILY SUMMARY - " DELIMITED SIZE
               ws_current_date DELIMITED SIZE
               INTO report_line
        WRITE report_line
        PERFORM 6110-write_totals
    """
    print("GENERATING DAILY SUMMARY...")
    data.REPORT_LINE = ""
    data.REPORT_LINE = "mega_enterprise DAILY SUMMARY - " + data.WS_CURRENT_DATE
    print(data.REPORT_LINE)  # Simulate WRITE report_line
    write_totals_C2(data)

def write_totals_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """6110-write_totals
        MOVE ws_total_deposits TO ws_formatted_amount
        STRING "TOTAL DEPOSITS: " DELIMITED SIZE
               ws_formatted_amount DELIMITED SIZE
               INTO report_line
        WRITE report_line
        
        MOVE ws_total_withdrawals TO ws_formatted_amount
        STRING "TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: " DELIMITED SIZE
               ws_formatted_amount DELIMITED SIZE
               INTO report_line
        WRITE report_line
        
        MOVE ws_total_loans TO ws_formatted_amount
        STRING "TOTAL LOANS: " DELIMITED SIZE
               ws_formatted_amount DELIMITED SIZE
               INTO report_line
        WRITE report_line
    """
    data.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(data.WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS)
    data.REPORT_LINE = "TOTAL DEPOSITS: " + data.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT
    print(data.REPORT_LINE)  # Simulate WRITE report_line

    data.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(data.WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS)
    data.REPORT_LINE = "TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: " + data.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT
    print(data.REPORT_LINE)  # Simulate WRITE report_line

    data.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(data.WS_TOTAL_LOANS)
    data.REPORT_LINE = "TOTAL LOANS: " + data.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT
    print(data.REPORT_LINE)  # Simulate WRITE report_line

def write_transaction_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    data.TRAN_TIMESTAMP = data.WS_CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
    data.TRAN_TYPE = 'DEP'
    data.TRAN_AMOUNT = data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    data.TRAN_STATUS = 'C'
    print("WRITE transaction_record")  # Simulate WRITE transaction_record

def write_audit_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    data.AUD_TIMESTAMP = data.WS_CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
    print("WRITE audit_record")  # Simulate WRITE audit_record

def format_date_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """8300-format_date
        STRING ws_temp_date(1:4) DELIMITED SIZE
               '-' DELIMITED SIZE
               ws_temp_date(5:2) DELIMITED SIZE
               '-' DELIMITED SIZE
               ws_temp_date(7:2) DELIMITED SIZE
               INTO ws_formatted_date
    """
    data.WS_FORMATTED_DATE = f"{data.WS_TEMP_DATE[0:4]}-{data.WS_TEMP_DATE[4:6]}-{data.WS_TEMP_DATE[6:8]}"

def validate_account_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """8400-validate_account
        SET ws_valid TO TRUE
        IF acct_id  = None  # TODO: was SPACES
            SET ws_invalid TO TRUE
        
    """
    data.WS_VALID = True
    if data.ACCT_ID == "":
        data.WS_INVALID = True

def calculate_tax_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    match True:
        case data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= data.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX:
            data.WS_CALC_TAX = data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT * data.WS_BRACKET_1_RATE
        case data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= data.WS_BRACKET_2_MAX:
            data.WS_CALC_TAX = (data.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * data.WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + \
                                 ((data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT - data.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * data.WS_BRACKET_2_RATE)
        case data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= data.WS_BRACKET_3_MAX:
            data.WS_CALC_TAX = (data.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * data.WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + \
                                 ((data.WS_BRACKET_2_MAX - data.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * data.WS_BRACKET_2_RATE) + \
                                 ((data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT - data.WS_BRACKET_2_MAX) * data.WS_BRACKET_3_RATE)
        case _:
            data.WS_CALC_TAX = data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT * data.WS_BRACKET_5_RATE

def termination_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """9000-TERMINATION
        PERFORM 9100-close_files
        PERFORM 9200-display_statistics
        DISPLAY "mega_enterprise SYSTEM TERMINATED NORMALLY"
    """
    close_files_C2()
    display_statistics_C2(data)
    print("mega_enterprise SYSTEM TERMINATED NORMALLY")

def close_files_C2() -> None:
    """9100-close_files
        CLOSE customer_master
        CLOSE account_master
        CLOSE loan_master
        CLOSE insurance_master
        CLOSE investment_master
        CLOSE transaction_log
        CLOSE audit_trail
        CLOSE report_file
    """
    print("CLOSE customer_master")  # Simulate CLOSE customer_master
    print("CLOSE account_master")  # Simulate CLOSE account_master
    print("CLOSE loan_master")  # Simulate CLOSE loan_master
    print("CLOSE insurance_master")  # Simulate CLOSE insurance_master
    print("CLOSE investment_master")  # Simulate CLOSE investment_master
    print("CLOSE transaction_log")  # Simulate CLOSE transaction_log
    print("CLOSE audit_trail")  # Simulate CLOSE audit_trail
    print("CLOSE report_file")  # Simulate CLOSE report_file

def display_statistics_C2(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """9200-display_statistics
        DISPLAY "============================================"
        DISPLAY "       PROCESSING STATISTICS                "
        DISPLAY "============================================"
        MOVE ws_cust_count TO ws_formatted_count
        DISPLAY "CUSTOMERS PROCESSED:    " ws_formatted_count
        MOVE ws_acct_count TO ws_formatted_count
        DISPLAY "ACCOUNTS PROCESSED:     " ws_formatted_count
        MOVE ws_tran_count TO ws_formatted_count
        DISPLAY "TRANSACTIONS PROCESSED: " ws_formatted_count
        MOVE ws_loan_count TO ws_formatted_count
        DISPLAY "LOANS PROCESSED:        " ws_formatted_count
        MOVE ws_error_count TO ws_formatted_count
        DISPLAY "ERRORS ENCOUNTERED:     " ws_formatted_count
        DISPLAY "============================================"
        MOVE ws_total_deposits TO ws_formatted_amount
        DISPLAY "TOTAL DEPOSITS:    " ws_formatted_amount
        MOVE ws_total_withdrawals TO ws_formatted_amount
        DISPLAY "TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: " ws_formatted_amount
        MOVE ws_total_interest TO ws_formatted_amount
        DISPLAY "TOTAL INTEREST:    " ws_formatted_amount
        MOVE ws_total_fees TO ws_formatted_amount
        DISPLAY "TOTAL FEES:        " ws_formatted_amount
        DISPLAY "============================================"
    """
    print("============================================")
    print("       PROCESSING STATISTICS                ")
    print("============================================")
    data.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT = str(data.WS_CUST_COUNT)
    print("CUSTOMERS PROCESSED:    ", data.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT)
    data.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT = str(data.WS_ACCT_COUNT)
    print("ACCOUNTS PROCESSED:     ", data.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT)
    data.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT = str(data.WS_TRAN_COUNT)
    print("TRANSACTIONS PROCESSED: ", data.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT)
    data.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT = str(data.WS_LOAN_COUNT)
    print("LOANS PROCESSED:        ", data.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT)
    data.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT = str(data.WS_ERROR_COUNT)
    print("ERRORS ENCOUNTERED:     ", data.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT)
    print("============================================")
    data.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(data.WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS)
    print("TOTAL DEPOSITS:    ", data.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT)
    data.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(data.WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS)
    print("TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: ", data

@dataclass
class CustomerMaster:
    """Represents the customer_master record."""
    cust_id: Optional[str] = None
    cust_name: Optional[str] = None
    cust_last_name: Optional[str] = None
    cust_state: Optional[str] = None
    cust_credit_score: Optional[int] = None
    cust_total_balance: Optional[Decimal] = None
    cust_total_loans: Optional[Decimal] = None
    cust_total_investments: Optional[Decimal] = None
    cust_last_activity: Optional[int] = None

@dataclass
class Loan:
    """Represents a LOAN record."""
    loan_delinquent: Optional[bool] = None

@dataclass
class Account:
    """Represents an ACCOUNT record."""
    acct_balance: Optional[Decimal] = None

def customer_segmentation_C3() -> None:
    """9310-customer_segmentation."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF, CUSTOMER_MASTER
    print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        # Simulate reading customer_master record
        # In a real scenario, this would involve file I/O or database query
        # For example:
        # try:
        #     CUSTOMER_MASTER = read_next_customer_record()
        # except StopIteration:
        #     WS_EOF = True
        #     CUSTOMER_MASTER = None # or initialize to a default CustomerMaster object
        #     continue
        
        #Simulate end of file
        WS_EOF = True
        
        if not WS_EOF:
            calculate_clv_C3()
            assign_segment_C3()

def calculate_clv_C3() -> None:
    """9311-calculate_clv."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, CUSTOMER_MASTER, WS_SAVINGS_RATE, WS_PERSONAL_RATE
    WS_CALC_RESULT = (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_balance * WS_SAVINGS_RATE) + (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_loans * WS_PERSONAL_RATE) + (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_investments * Decimal('0.01'))

def assign_segment_C3() -> None:
    """9312-assign_segment."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, WS_TEMP_CODE
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > 10000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'PLATINUM'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 5000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'GOLD'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 1000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'SILVER'
    else:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'BRONZE'

def sweep_accounts_C3() -> None:
    """9632-sweep_accounts."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT, ACCT_BALANCE, ACCT_MIN_BALANCE, WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS
    if ACCT_BALANCE > ACCT_MIN_BALANCE:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = ACCT_BALANCE - ACCT_MIN_BALANCE
        ACCT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT
        WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def extract_data_C3() -> None:
    """A110-extract_data."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF, CUSTOMER_MASTER, WS_PROCESS_COUNT
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        # Simulate reading customer_master record
        # In a real scenario, this would involve file I/O or database query
        # For example:
        # try:
        #     CUSTOMER_MASTER = read_next_customer_record()
        # except StopIteration:
        #     WS_EOF = True
        #     CUSTOMER_MASTER = None # or initialize to a default CustomerMaster object
        #     continue
        
        WS_EOF = True #Simulate end of file
        
        if not WS_EOF:
            WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1

WS_TOTAL_LOANS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_CALC_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_TOTAL_FEES: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

LOAN_CURRENT_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

WS_FORMATTED_COUNT: str = ""
WS_CUST_COUNT: int = 0

def c100_transaction_monitoring_c4() -> None:
    """C100-transaction_monitoring
    """
    global WS_NOT_EOF
    global WS_EOF
    global TRANSACTION_LOG
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        # Simulate reading from TRANSACTION_LOG - replace with actual read logic
        if not isinstance(TRANSACTION_LOG, TransactionLog):  # Assuming no more data
            WS_EOF = True
        else:
            c110_rule_based_detection_c4()
            c120_behavior_analysis_c4()
            c130_network_analysis_c4()
            WS_EOF = True

def c110_rule_based_detection_c4() -> None:
    """C110-rule_based_detection

    global TRAN_AMOUNT
    if TRAN_AMOUNT >= 10000:
        c111_flag_ctr_c4()
    if TRAN_AMOUNT >= 5000 and TRAN_AMOUNT < 10000:
        c112_check_structuring_c4()

def d110_classification_c4() -> None:
    """D110-CLASSIFICATION
    """
    global CUST_CREDIT_SCORE
    global CUST_RISK_RATING
    if CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 750:
        CUST_RISK_RATING = 'A'
    elif CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 650:
        CUST_RISK_RATING = 'B'
    elif CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 550:
        CUST_RISK_RATING = 'C'
    else:
        CUST_RISK_RATING = 'D'

def d120_regression_c4() -> None:
    """D120-REGRESSION
    """
    global WS_CALC_RESULT
    global CUST_CREDIT_SCORE
    global CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE
    global CUST_TOTAL_LOANS
    WS_CALC_RESULT = (CUST_CREDIT_SCORE * 10) + \
                       (CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE / 1000) - \
                       (CUST_TOTAL_LOANS / 2000)

def initialization_1000_C5():
    """COBOL logic"""

    open_files_1100_C5()
    read_parameters_1200_C5()
    initialize_tables_1300_C5()

def open_files_1100_C5():
    """OPEN INPUT  customer_file
       OPEN INPUT  account_file
       OPEN INPUT  transaction_file
       OPEN OUTPUT report_file
       OPEN OUTPUT error_file
       OPEN I-O    master_file
       IF ws_file_status NOT = '00'
          MOVE 'FILE OPEN ERROR' TO ws_error_msg
          PERFORM 9500-abort_process
       
    global CUSTOMER_FILE, ACCOUNT_FILE, TRANSACTION_FILE, REPORT_FILE, ERROR_FILE, MASTER_FILE, WS_FILE_STATUS, WS_ERROR_MSG
    try:
        CUSTOMER_FILE = open("CUSTOMER_FILE", "r")
        ACCOUNT_FILE = open("ACCOUNT_FILE", "r")
        TRANSACTION_FILE = open("TRANSACTION_FILE", "r")
        REPORT_FILE = open("REPORT_FILE", "w")
        ERROR_FILE = open("ERROR_FILE", "w")
        MASTER_FILE = open("MASTER_FILE", "r+")
        WS_FILE_STATUS = '00'  # Assuming open was successful
    except Exception as e:
        WS_FILE_STATUS = '99'  # Assuming error code
        WS_ERROR_MSG = "FILE OPEN ERROR"
        abort_process_9500_C5()
    if WS_FILE_STATUS != '00':
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'FILE OPEN ERROR'
        abort_process_9500_C5()

def read_parameters_1200_C5():
    # COBOL reference preserved
       ACCEPT ws_param_time FROM TIME
       MOVE 'batch_001' TO ws_job_id
       MOVE 'PRODUCTION' TO ws_env_type
       COMPUTE ws_process_date = 
          FUNCTION integer_of_date(ws_param_date)."""
    global WS_PARAM_DATE, WS_PARAM_TIME, WS_JOB_ID, WS_ENV_TYPE, WS_PROCESS_DATE
    today = datetime.now()
    WS_PARAM_DATE = today.strftime("%Y%m%d")
    WS_PARAM_TIME = today.strftime("%H%M%S")
    WS_JOB_ID = 'batch_001'
    WS_ENV_TYPE = 'PRODUCTION'
    WS_PROCESS_DATE = int(WS_PARAM_DATE)  # Approximation of integer_of_date

def initialize_tables_1300_C5():
    # COBOL reference preserved
          UNTIL ws_tbl_idx > 100
          INITIALIZE rate_table_entry(ws_tbl_idx)
          MOVE ZEROES TO rt_rate(ws_tbl_idx)
          MOVE SPACES TO rt_code(ws_tbl_idx)
       
       PERFORM VARYING ws_tbl_idx FROM 1 BY 1
          UNTIL ws_tbl_idx > 50
          INITIALIZE branch_table_entry(ws_tbl_idx)."""COBOL logic"""
    global WS_TBL_IDX, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_REF_RECORD, RT_CODE, RT_RATE, REFERENCE_FILE, RATE_TABLE_ENTRY
    WS_TBL_IDX = 1
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    try:
        with open("REFERENCE_FILE", "r") as REFERENCE_FILE:
            while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y' and WS_TBL_IDX <= 100:
                line = REFERENCE_FILE.readline().strip()
                if not line:
                    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
                else:
                    WS_REF_RECORD = line
                    WS_REF_CODE = line[:5]  # Assuming ws_ref_code is first 5 chars
                    WS_REF_RATE = Decimal(line[5:])  # Assuming ws_ref_rate is rest
                    RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX - 1].rt_code  = None  # TODO: was WS_REF_CODE
                    RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX - 1].rt_rate  = None  # TODO: was WS_REF_RATE
                    WS_TBL_IDX += 1
    except FileNotFoundError:
        WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # Handle file not found as EOF

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def process_transactions_2000_C5():
    # COBOL reference preserved
          AT END
             MOVE 'Y' TO ws_eof_flag
          NOT AT END
             ADD 1 TO ws_trans_count
             PERFORM 2100-validate_transaction
             IF ws_valid_flag = 'Y'
                PERFORM 2200-process_by_type

                PERFORM 2900-handle_error
             
       
    global TRANSACTION_FILE, WS_TRANSACTION_REC, WS_TRANS_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_VALID_FLAG
    try:
        line = TRANSACTION_FILE.readline().strip()
        if not line:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
            return
        WS_TRANSACTION_REC = line  # Store the line in WS_TRANSACTION_REC
        WS_TRANS_COUNT += 1
        validate_transaction_2100_C5()
        if WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':
            process_by_type_2200_C5()
        else:
            handle_error_2900_C5()
    except Exception as e:
        WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

def validate_transaction_2100_C5():
    # COBOL reference preserved
       IF txn_account_id = SPACES OR low_values
          MOVE 'N' TO ws_valid_flag
          MOVE 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID' TO ws_error_msg
          EXIT PARAGRAPH
       
       IF txn_amount IS NOT NUMERIC
          MOVE 'N' TO ws_valid_flag
          MOVE 'INVALID AMOUNT' TO ws_error_msg
          EXIT PARAGRAPH
       
       IF txn_type NOT = 'D' AND txn_type NOT = 'W'
          AND txn_type NOT = 'T' AND txn_type NOT = 'I'
          MOVE 'N' TO ws_valid_flag
          MOVE 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE' TO ws_error_msg
       
       PERFORM 2150-validate_account_exists
       PERFORM 2160-validate_business_rules."""COBOL logic"""IF txn_type = 'W'
          IF txn_amount > ws_account_balance
             MOVE 'N' TO ws_valid_flag
             MOVE 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS' TO ws_error_msg
          
       
       IF txn_amount > 1000000
          MOVE 'N' TO ws_valid_flag
          MOVE 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT' TO ws_error_msg
       
    global TXN_TYPE, TXN_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_VALID_FLAG, WS_ERROR_MSG
    if TXN_TYPE == 'W':
        if TXN_AMOUNT > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
            WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
            WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'

    if TXN_AMOUNT > 1000000:
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT'

def update_account_2350_C5():
    # COBOL reference preserved
       MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO acct_last_update
       REWRITE account_record
       IF ws_file_status NOT = '00'
          MOVE 'UPDATE FAILED' TO ws_error_msg
          PERFORM 2900-handle_error
       
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, ACCT_BALANCE, ACCT_LAST_UPDATE, ACCOUNT_RECORD, WS_FILE_STATUS, WS_ERROR_MSG
    ACCT_BALANCE  = None  # TODO: was WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    ACCT_LAST_UPDATE = datetime.now()
    try:
        # Assuming a method to update the record in MASTER_FILE by ACCT_ID
        rewrite_account_record(ACCT_ID, ACCT_BALANCE, ACCT_LAST_UPDATE)
        WS_FILE_STATUS = '00' #Assuming successful
    except Exception:
        WS_FILE_STATUS = '99'  # Assuming error code
    if WS_FILE_STATUS != '00':
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'UPDATE FAILED'
        handle_error_2900_C5()

def calculate_pmi_C7(ws_ltv_ratio: Decimal, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """10335-calculate_pmi
    """
    match True:
        case ws_ltv_ratio > 95:
            ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0125') / 12
        case ws_ltv_ratio > 90:
            ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0100') / 12
        case ws_ltv_ratio > 85:
            ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0075') / 12
        case _:
            ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0050') / 12
    return ws_pmi_amount

def evaluate_history_C7(ws_late_90_days: int, ws_late_60_days: int, ws_late_30_days: int, ws_risk_score: int) -> tuple[int, str, str, str]:
    """10340-evaluate_history
    """
    ws_factor_1 = ''
    ws_factor_2 = ''
    ws_factor_3 = ''

    if ws_late_90_days > 0:
        ws_risk_score -= 50
        ws_factor_1 = 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY'
    if ws_late_60_days > 2:
        ws_risk_score -= 30
        ws_factor_2 = '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES'
    if ws_late_30_days > 5:
        ws_risk_score -= 20
        ws_factor_3 = 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES'

    return ws_risk_score, ws_factor_1, ws_factor_2, ws_factor_3

def calculate_final_risk_C7(ws_risk_score: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, str]:
    """10350-calculate_final_risk
    """
    ws_risk_score = ws_risk_score / 4
    match True:
        case ws_risk_score >= 80:
            ws_risk_category = 'LOW RISK'
        case ws_risk_score >= 60:
            ws_risk_category = 'MODERATE'
        case ws_risk_score >= 40:
            ws_risk_category = 'ELEVATED'
        case _:
            ws_risk_category = 'HIGH RISK'
    return ws_risk_score, ws_risk_category

def determine_approval_C7(ws_credit_tier: str, ws_risk_category: str, ws_dti_ratio: Decimal, ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_base_rate: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str, Decimal]:
    """10400-determine_approval
    """
    ws_approval_status = ''
    ws_conditions = ''
    ws_approved_amount = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_approved_rate = Decimal('0.00')

    if ws_credit_tier == 'F':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_risk_category == 'HIGH RISK':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_dti_ratio > 50:
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate

    ws_approval_status = 'A'
    ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate = calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws_loan_amount, ws_base_rate, ws_credit_tier, ws_risk_category)
    return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate

def calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_base_rate: Decimal, ws_credit_tier: str, ws_risk_category: str) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal]:
    """10450-calculate_approved_terms
    """
    ws_approved_amount = ws_loan_amount
    match ws_credit_tier:
        case 'A':
            ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.00')
        case 'B':
            ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.50')
        case 'C':
            ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('1.50')
        case 'D':
            ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('3.00')
        case _:
            ws_approved_rate = Decimal('0.00')

    if ws_risk_category == 'ELEVATED':
        ws_approved_rate += Decimal('0.50')

    return ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate

def generate_loan_terms_C7(ws_approved_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """10500-generate_loan_terms
    """
    ws_loan_interest_rate = ws_approved_rate
    ws_monthly_rate = ws_loan_interest_rate / 1200
    ws_compound_factor = (1 + ws_monthly_rate) ** ws_loan_term_months
    ws_loan_monthly_pmt = ws_loan_amount * ws_monthly_rate * ws_compound_factor / (ws_compound_factor - 1)
    ws_loan_principal_bal = ws_loan_amount
    return ws_loan_interest_rate, ws_monthly_rate, ws_compound_factor, ws_loan_monthly_pmt

def create_amortization_C7(ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_monthly_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, loan_mortgage: LoanMortgage, ws_property_tax: Decimal, ws_insurance_premium: Decimal, ws_pmi_amount: Decimal) -> list[AmortizationEntry]:
    """10600-create_amortization
    """
    ws_running_balance = ws_loan_amount
    ws_payment_date = date.today()
    ws_payment_month = ws_payment_date.month
    ws_payment_year = ws_payment_date.year
    amortization_schedule: list[AmortizationEntry] = []

    for ws_amort_idx in range(1, ws_loan_term_months + 1):
        amortization_entry = calculate_payment_split_C7(ws_amort_idx, ws_running_balance, ws_monthly_rate, ws_loan_monthly_pmt, loan_mortgage, ws_property_tax, ws_insurance_premium, ws_pmi_amount, ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year)
        ws_running_balance = amortization_entry.balance
        ws_payment_month = (amortization_entry.payment_date // 100) % 100
        ws_payment_year = amortization_entry.payment_date // 10000
        amortization_schedule.append(amortization_entry)

    return amortization_schedule

def calculate_payment_split_C7(ws_amort_idx: int, ws_running_balance: Decimal, ws_monthly_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, loan_mortgage: LoanMortgage, ws_property_tax: Decimal, ws_insurance_premium: Decimal, ws_pmi_amount: Decimal, ws_payment_month: int, ws_payment_year: int) -> AmortizationEntry:
    """10650-calculate_payment_split
    """
    amort_interest = ws_running_balance * ws_monthly_rate
    amort_principal = ws_loan_monthly_pmt - amort_interest
    ws_running_balance -= amort_principal

    amortization_entry = AmortizationEntry()
    amortization_entry.interest = amort_interest
    amortization_entry.principal = amort_principal
    amortization_entry.balance = ws_running_balance
    amortization_entry.payment_num = ws_amort_idx
    amortization_entry.payment_amt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt

    if loan_mortgage.loan_mortgage:
        amort_escrow = (ws_property_tax + ws_insurance_premium) / 12
        amort_total_pmt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt + amort_escrow + ws_pmi_amount
        amortization_entry.escrow = amort_escrow
    else:
        amort_total_pmt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt
    amortization_entry.total_pmt = amort_total_pmt

    amortization_entry.payment_date, ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year = advance_payment_date_C7(ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year)
    return amortization_entry

def advance_payment_date_C7(ws_payment_month: int, ws_payment_year: int) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    """10660-advance_payment_date
    """
    ws_payment_month += 1
    if ws_payment_month > 12:
        ws_payment_month = 1
        ws_payment_year += 1

    amort_payment_date = ws_payment_year * 10000 + ws_payment_month * 100 + 1
    return amort_payment_date, ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year

def finalize_loan_C7(ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_loan_id: str, ws_loan_type: str, ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_loan_interest_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal):
    """10700-finalize_loan
    """
    ws_loan_start_date = int(date.today().strftime('%Y%m%d'))
    ws_loan_end_date = ws_loan_start_date + (ws_loan_term_months * 30)
    ws_loan_status = 'A'
    create_loan_record_C7(ws_loan_id, ws_loan_type, ws_loan_amount, ws_loan_interest_rate, ws_loan_monthly_pmt, ws_loan_start_date, ws_loan_status)
    disburse_funds_C7(ws_loan_amount)
    send_confirmation_C7()

    # WRITE loan_record FROM ws_loan_record. Placeholder for actual write operation

def portfolio_management_C7():
    """11000-portfolio_management
    """
    load_portfolio_C7()

    calculate_values_C7()
    rebalance_check_C7()
    generate_statements_C7()

def load_portfolio_C7():
    """11100-load_portfolio
    """
    ws_hold_idx = 1
    ws_eof_flag = ''
    ws_holding = []
    ws_holdings_count = 0

    while ws_hold_idx <= 100 and ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            # READ holdings_file INTO ws_holding_rec
            ws_holding_rec = read_holdings_file()
            ws_holding.append(ws_holding_rec)
            ws_hold_idx += 1
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    ws_holdings_count = ws_hold_idx - 1

def update_market_prices_C7():
    """11200-update_market_prices
    """COBOL logic"""11250-get_quote
    """
    quote_request = QuoteRequest(quote_request_symbol=ws_quote_symbol)
    quote_response = call_getquote(quote_request) # Assuming call_getquote is defined elsewhere

    if quote_response.quote_response_status == 'OK':
        ws_quote_price = quote_response.quote_last_price
    else:
        ws_quote_price = Decimal('0.00')

    return ws_quote_price

def calculate_values_C7():
    """11300-calculate_values
    """
    ws_total_value = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_cost_basis = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_unrealized_gain = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_holdings_count = 0 # Placeholder
    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain = calculate_holding_value_C7(ws_hold_idx, ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain)

def calculate_holding_value_C7(ws_hold_idx: int, ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_cost_basis: Decimal, ws_unrealized_gain: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """11350-calculate_holding_value
    """
    hold_shares = Decimal('0.00')  # Placeholder
    hold_current_price = Decimal('0.00')  # Placeholder
    hold_cost_per_share = Decimal('0.00') # Placeholder

    hold_market_value = hold_shares * hold_current_price
    ws_hold_cost = hold_shares * hold_cost_per_share
    hold_gain_loss = hold_market_value - ws_hold_cost

    if ws_hold_cost > 0:
        hold_pct_change = (hold_gain_loss / ws_hold_cost) * 100
    else:
        hold_pct_change = Decimal('0.00')

    ws_total_value += hold_market_value
    ws_cost_basis += ws_hold_cost
    ws_unrealized_gain += hold_gain_loss

    return ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain

def rebalance_check_C7():
    """11400-rebalance_check
    """
    ws_rebalance_needed, ws_stocks_diff, ws_bonds_diff = calculate_current_allocation_C7()
    ws_rebalance_needed = compare_to_target_C7(ws_stocks_diff, ws_bonds_diff, ws_rebalance_needed)
    if ws_rebalance_needed == 'Y':
        generate_rebalance_trades_C7(ws_stocks_diff)

def calculate_current_allocation_C7() -> tuple[str, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """11410-calculate_current_allocation
    """
    ws_stocks_value = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_bonds_value = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_cash_value = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_total_value = Decimal('0.00') # Placeholder
    ws_holdings_count = 0 # Placeholder for ws_holdings_count from previous functions
    ws_stocks_pct = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_bonds_pct = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_cash_pct = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_rebalance_needed = 'N'

    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        hold_type = '' # Placeholder for hold_type(ws_hold_idx)
        hold_market_value = Decimal('0.00') # Placeholder for hold_market_value(ws_hold_idx)
        match hold_type:
            case 'STK':
                ws_stocks_value += hold_market_value
            case 'BND':
                ws_bonds_value += hold_market_value
            case 'CSH':
                ws_cash_value += hold_market_value

    ws_stocks_pct = (ws_stocks_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    ws_bonds_pct = (ws_bonds_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    ws_cash_pct = (ws_cash_value / ws_total_value) * 100

    return ws_rebalance_needed, ws_stocks_pct, ws_bonds_pct

def compare_to_target_C7(ws_stocks_pct: Decimal, ws_bonds_pct: Decimal, ws_rebalance_needed: str) -> str:
    """11420-compare_to_target
    """
    ws_target_stocks_pct = Decimal('0.00') # Placeholder
    ws_target_bonds_pct = Decimal('0.00') # Placeholder
    ws_stocks_diff = ws_stocks_pct - ws_target_stocks_pct
    ws_bonds_diff = ws_bonds_pct - ws_target_bonds_pct

    if abs(ws_stocks_diff) > 5:
        ws_rebalance_needed = 'Y'

    if abs(ws_bonds_diff) > 5:
        ws_rebalance_needed = 'Y'

    return ws_rebalance_needed

def generate_rebalance_trades_C7(ws_stocks_diff: Decimal):
    """11430-generate_rebalance_trades
    """
    ws_total_value = Decimal('0.00') # Placeholder

    if ws_stocks_diff > 0:
        ws_sell_amount = ws_total_value * ws_stocks_diff / 100
        create_sell_order_C7(ws_sell_amount)
    else:
        ws_buy_amount = ws_total_value * (0 - ws_stocks_diff) / 100
        create_buy_order_C7(ws_buy_amount)

def trade_execution_C7():
    """12000-trade_execution
    """
    ws_trade_symbol = '' # Placeholder
    ws_trade_shares = 0 # Placeholder
    order_flags = OrderFlags()
    ws_limit_price = Decimal('0.00') # Placeholder

    ws_order_valid, ws_reject_reason = validate_order_C7(ws_trade_symbol, ws_trade_shares, order_flags, ws_limit_price)
    if ws_order_valid == 'Y':
        trade_flags = TradeFlags()
        ws_estimated_price = Decimal('0.00') # Placeholder
        ws_available_cash = Decimal('0.00') # Placeholder
        ws_sufficient_flag, ws_reject_reason = check_funds_shares_C7(trade_flags, ws_trade_shares, ws_estimated_price, ws_available_cash, ws_trade_symbol)
        if ws_sufficient_flag == 'Y':
            ws_routing_type, ws_order_time = route_order_C7()
            ws_current_market_price = Decimal('0.00') # Placeholder
            ws_trade_status, ws_executed_price = execute_order_C7(order_flags, ws_limit_price, ws_current_market_price, trade_flags)
            settle_trade_C7(ws_trade_status, ws_executed_price)
        else:
            reject_order_C7(ws_reject_reason)

def validate_order_C7(ws_trade_symbol: str, ws_trade_shares: int, order_flags: OrderFlags, ws_limit_price: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """12100-validate_order
    """
    ws_order_valid = 'Y'
    ws_reject_reason = ''

    if ws_trade_symbol == '':
        ws_order_valid = 'N'
        ws_reject_reason = 'SYMBOL REQUIRED'
        return ws_order_valid, ws_reject_reason

    if ws_trade_shares <= 0:
        ws_order_valid = 'N'
        ws_reject_reason = 'INVALID QUANTITY'
        return ws_order_valid, ws_reject_reason

    if order_flags.order_limit or order_flags.order_stop_limit:
        if ws_limit_price <= 0:
            ws_order_valid = 'N'
            ws_reject_reason = 'LIMIT PRICE REQUIRED'

    return ws_order_valid, ws_reject_reason

def check_funds_shares_C7(trade_flags: TradeFlags, ws_trade_shares: int, ws_estimated_price: Decimal, ws_available_cash: Decimal, ws_trade_symbol: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """12200-check_funds_shares
    """
    ws_sufficient_flag = 'Y'
    ws_reject_reason = ''

    if trade_flags.trade_buy:
        ws_required_funds = ws_trade_shares * ws_estimated_price
        if ws_required_funds > ws_available_cash:
            ws_sufficient_flag = 'N'
            ws_reject_reason = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
            return ws_sufficient_flag, ws_reject_reason

    if trade_flags.trade_sell:
        ws_current_shares = check_share_position_C7(ws_trade_symbol)
        if ws_current_shares < ws_trade_shares:
            ws_sufficient_flag = 'N'
            ws_reject_reason = 'INSUFFICIENT SHARES'

    return ws_sufficient_flag, ws_reject_reason

def check_share_position_C7(ws_trade_symbol: str) -> Decimal:
    """12250-check_share_position
    """
    ws_current_shares = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_holdings_count = 0 # Placeholder

    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        hold_symbol = '' # Placeholder for hold_symbol(ws_hold_idx)
        hold_shares = Decimal('0.00')  # Placeholder for hold_shares(ws_hold_idx)

        if hold_symbol == ws_trade_symbol:
            ws_current_shares += hold_shares

    return ws_current_shares

def route_order_C7() -> tuple[str, int]:
    """12300-route_order
    """
    ws_trade_amount = Decimal('0.00') # Placeholder
    match True:
        case ws_trade_amount > 100000:
            ws_routing_type = 'ALGO'
        case ws_trade_amount > 10000:
            ws_routing_type = 'SMART'
        case _:
            ws_routing_type = 'DIRECT'

    ws_order_time = int(date.today().strftime('%Y%m%d'))

    return ws_routing_type, ws_order_time

def execute_order_C7(order_flags: OrderFlags, ws_limit_price: Decimal, ws_current_market_price: Decimal, trade_flags: TradeFlags) -> tuple[str, Decimal]:
    """12400-execute_order
    """
    ws_executed_price = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_trade_status = ''

    if order_flags.order_market:
        ws_executed_price, ws_trade_status = market_order_C7(ws_current_market_price)
    elif order_flags.order_limit:
        ws_executed_price, ws_trade_status = limit_order_C7(trade_flags, ws_current_market_price, ws_limit_price)
    elif order_flags.order_stop:
        ws_executed_price, ws_trade_status = stop_order_C7(trade_flags, ws_current_market_price)
    else:
        ws_executed_price, ws_trade_status = stop_limit_order_C7(ws_current_market_price, ws_limit_price)

    return ws_trade_status, ws_executed_price

def market_order_C7(ws_current_market_price: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, str]:
    """12410-market_order
    """COBOL logic"""12420-limit_order
    """
    ws_executed_price = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_trade_status = ''

    if trade_flags.trade_buy:
        if ws_current_market_price <= ws_limit_price:
            ws_executed_price = ws_current_market_price
            ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
        else:

            pass

def _13220_calc_auto_premium_C8() -> None:
    """13220-calc_auto_premium."""
    global WS_BASE_PREMIUM, WS_DRIVER_AGE, WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR, WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE
    global WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR, WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE, WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM, WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM
    
    match WS_DRIVER_AGE:
        case age if 6 <= age <= 10:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM += 100
        case _:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM += 50
    
    if WS_DRIVER_AGE < 25:
        WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5")
        
    if WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 0:
        WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE = WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR * 200
        WS_BASE_PREMIUM += WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE
        
    if WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR > 0:
        WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE = WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR * 100
        WS_BASE_PREMIUM += WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE
        
    WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM  = None  # TODO: was WS_BASE_PREMIUM
    WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / 12

def _13230_calc_home_premium_C8() -> None:
    """13230-calc_home_premium."""
    global WS_BASE_PREMIUM, WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT, WS_HOME_AGE, WS_FLOOD_ZONE, WS_SECURITY_SYSTEM
    global WS_DEDUCTIBLE, WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT, WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM, WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM
    
    WS_BASE_PREMIUM = WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.003")
    
    match WS_HOME_AGE:
        case age if 0 <= age <= 10:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9")
        case age if 11 <= age <= 25:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0")
        case age if 26 <= age <= 50:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.2")
        case _:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5")
            
    if WS_FLOOD_ZONE == 'Y':
        WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5")
        
    if WS_SECURITY_SYSTEM == 'Y':
        WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9")
        
    WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT = WS_DEDUCTIBLE / 1000 * 50
    WS_BASE_PREMIUM -= WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT
    
    if WS_BASE_PREMIUM < 200:
        WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal("200.00")
        
    WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM  = None  # TODO: was WS_BASE_PREMIUM
    WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / 12

def _13240_calc_health_premium_C8() -> None:
    """13240-calc_health_premium."""
    global WS_BASE_PREMIUM, WS_INSURED_AGE, WS_PLAN_TYPE, WS_FAMILY_PLAN, WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM, WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM
    
    WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal("300.00")
    
    match WS_INSURED_AGE:
        case age if 0 <= age <= 18:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.5")
        case age if 19 <= age <= 30:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0")
        case age if 31 <= age <= 40:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.3")
        case age if 41 <= age <= 50:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.6")
        case age if 51 <= age <= 60:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.0")
        case _:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.8")
            
    match WS_PLAN_TYPE:
        case 'BRONZE':
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.8")
        case 'SILVER':
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0")
        case 'GOLD':
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.3")
        case 'PLATINUM':
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.6")
            
    if WS_FAMILY_PLAN == 'Y':
        WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.5")
        
    WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM  = None  # TODO: was WS_BASE_PREMIUM
    WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM * 12

def _13300_underwriting_C8() -> None:
    """13300-UNDERWRITING."""
    _13310_evaluate_risk_factors_C8()
    _13320_check_medical_history_C8()
    _13330_verify_information_C8()
    _13340_determine_decision_C8()

def _13310_evaluate_risk_factors_C8() -> None:
    """13310-evaluate_risk_factors."""
    global WS_RISK_POINTS, WS_BMI, WS_SMOKER_FLAG, WS_HAZARDOUS_OCCUPATION, WS_DRIVER_AGE, WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR, POLICY_LIFE, POLICY_AUTO
    
    WS_RISK_POINTS = 0
    
    if POLICY_LIFE:
        if WS_BMI > 30:
            WS_RISK_POINTS += 10
        if WS_SMOKER_FLAG == 'Y':
            WS_RISK_POINTS += 25
        if WS_HAZARDOUS_OCCUPATION == 'Y':
            WS_RISK_POINTS += 15
            
    if POLICY_AUTO:
        if WS_DRIVER_AGE < 21:
            WS_RISK_POINTS += 20
        if WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 1:
            WS_RISK_POINTS += 15

def _13320_check_medical_history_C8() -> None:
    """13320-check_medical_history."""
    global WS_RISK_POINTS, WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS, WS_CONDITION_POINTS, WS_RECENT_HOSPITALIZATION, WS_PRESCRIPTION_COUNT
    
    if WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS > 0:
        WS_CONDITION_POINTS = WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS * 5
        WS_RISK_POINTS += None  # TODO: was WS_CONDITION_POINTS
        
    if WS_RECENT_HOSPITALIZATION == 'Y':
        WS_RISK_POINTS += 10
        
    if WS_PRESCRIPTION_COUNT > 5:
        WS_RISK_POINTS += 5

def _13330_verify_information_C8() -> None:
    """13330-verify_information."""
    _13335_check_fraud_indicators_C8()
    _13336_validate_documents_C8()

def _13335_check_fraud_indicators_C8() -> None:
    """13335-check_fraud_indicators."""
    global WS_RISK_POINTS, WS_RECENT_CLAIMS, WS_FRAUD_FLAG, WS_ADDRESS_MISMATCH
    
    if WS_RECENT_CLAIMS > 3:
        WS_RISK_POINTS += 20
        WS_FRAUD_FLAG = 'Y'
        
    if WS_ADDRESS_MISMATCH == 'Y':
        WS_RISK_POINTS += 10

def _13336_validate_documents_C8() -> None:
    """13336-validate_documents."""
    global WS_DOC_MISSING, WS_UW_STATUS
    
    if WS_DOC_MISSING == 'Y':
        WS_UW_STATUS = 'PENDING'
    else:
        WS_UW_STATUS = 'COMPLETE'

def _13340_determine_decision_C8() -> None:
    """13340-determine_decision."""
    global WS_RISK_POINTS, WS_UW_DECISION, WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM
    
    match True:
        case bool(WS_RISK_POINTS > 50):
            WS_UW_DECISION = 'DECLINE'
        case bool(WS_RISK_POINTS > 30):
            WS_UW_DECISION = 'SUBSTANDARD'
            WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5")
        case bool(WS_RISK_POINTS > 15):
            WS_UW_DECISION = 'STANDARD'
        case _:
            WS_UW_DECISION = 'PREFERRED'
            WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9")

def _13400_issue_policy_C8() -> None:
    """13400-issue_policy."""
    global WS_UW_DECISION
    
    if WS_UW_DECISION != 'DECLINE':
        _13410_generate_policy_number_C8()
        _13420_create_policy_record_C8()
        _13430_set_beneficiaries_C8()
        _13440_send_policy_docs_C8()
    else:
        _13450_send_decline_letter_C8()

    WS_DATE_PART = datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    WS_TYPE_PART  = None  # TODO: was WS_POLICY_TYPE
    WS_RANDOM_PART = Decimal(str(random.random() * 99999))
    WS_POLICY_NUMBER = f"{WS_TYPE_PART}{WS_DATE_PART}{WS_RANDOM_PART}"

def _13430_set_beneficiaries_C8() -> None:
    """13430-set_beneficiaries."""
    global WS_BENEF_IDX, WS_BENEFICIARY_REC, BENEF_REC_POLICY, WS_POLICY_NUMBER, BENEF_REC_NAME, BENEF_REC_RELATION
    global BENEF_REC_PCT, BENEFICIARY_RECORD, BENEF_NAME, BENEF_RELATION, BENEF_PCT
    
    for WS_BENEF_IDX in range(1, 6):
        if WS_BENEF_IDX <= len(BENEF_NAME) and BENEF_NAME[WS_BENEF_IDX - 1].strip() != "":
            WS_BENEFICIARY_REC = BeneficiaryRecord()
            WS_BENEFICIARY_REC.BENEF_REC_POLICY  = None  # TODO: was WS_POLICY_NUMBER
            WS_BENEFICIARY_REC.BENEF_REC_NAME = BENEF_NAME[WS_BENEF_IDX - 1]
            WS_BENEFICIARY_REC.BENEF_REC_RELATION = BENEF_RELATION[WS_BENEF_IDX - 1]
            WS_BENEFICIARY_REC.BENEF_REC_PCT = BENEF_PCT[WS_BENEF_IDX - 1]
            BENEFICIARY_RECORD  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_REC
            #WRITE beneficiary_record FROM ws_beneficiary_rec. (Assuming this writes record)
            print("Writing beneficiary record:", BENEFICIARY_RECORD)

def _13522_check_policy_status_C8() -> None:
    """13522-check_policy_status."""
    global WS_POLICY_STATUS, WS_CLAIM_STATUS, WS_CLAIM_DENY_REASON
    
    if WS_POLICY_STATUS != 'A':
        WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'DENIED'
        WS_CLAIM_DENY_REASON = 'POLICY NOT ACTIVE'

def _13524_check_coverage_C8() -> None:
    """13524-check_coverage."""
    global WS_CLAIM_TYPE, WS_COVERED_PERILS, WS_CLAIM_STATUS, WS_CLAIM_DENY_REASON
    
    if WS_CLAIM_TYPE != WS_COVERED_PERILS:
        WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'DENIED'
        WS_CLAIM_DENY_REASON = 'NOT COVERED PERIL'

def _13526_check_deductible_C8() -> None:
    """13526-check_deductible."""
    global WS_CLAIM_AMOUNT, WS_DEDUCTIBLE, WS_CLAIM_STATUS, WS_CLAIM_DENY_REASON
    
    if WS_CLAIM_AMOUNT <= WS_DEDUCTIBLE:
        WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'DENIED'
        WS_CLAIM_DENY_REASON = 'BELOW DEDUCTIBLE'

def _13530_investigate_claim_C8() -> None:
    """13530-investigate_claim."""
    global WS_CLAIM_AMOUNT, WS_CLAIM_STATUS, WS_ADJUSTER_ID, WS_NOTES, WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT
    
    if WS_CLAIM_AMOUNT > 10000:
        WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'INVESTIGATION'
        _13535_assign_adjuster_C8()
    _13536_fraud_check_C8()

def sixteen_thousand_one_hundred_fifteen_C9(ws_pep_status: str, pep_match_score: int, ws_pep_score: int) -> None:
    """16115-process_pep
    """
    ws_pep_status = 'Y'
    ws_pep_score = pep_match_score
    return ws_pep_status, ws_pep_score

def sixteen_thousand_one_hundred_sixteen_C9(ws_customer_name: str, media_request: str, media_response: str, media_hits_found: int, ws_watchlist_hits: int) -> int:
    """16116-check_adverse_media
    """
    media_search_name = ws_customer_name
    # Assuming MEDIASRCH is an external function or API call
    # Here, we mock the call and assume media_hits_found is updated somehow
    # CALL 'MEDIASRCH' USING media_request media_response
    if media_hits_found > 0:
        ws_watchlist_hits += media_hits_found
    return ws_watchlist_hits

def sixteen_thousand_one_hundred_twenty_C9(ws_ofac_score: int, ws_pep_score: int, ws_match_score: Decimal, ws_watchlist_hits: int) -> Decimal:
    """16120-calculate_match_score
    """
    if ws_ofac_score > 0:
        ws_match_score += ws_ofac_score
    if ws_pep_score > 0:
        ws_match_score += ws_pep_score
    ws_match_score = ws_match_score / ws_watchlist_hits
    return ws_match_score

def sixteen_thousand_one_hundred_thirty_C9(ws_match_score: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """16130-determine_disposition
    """
    ws_sar_required = 'N'
    ws_case_status = ''
    match True:
        case ws_match_score >= 90:
            ws_match_type = 'CONFIRMED'
            ws_sar_required = 'Y'
        case ws_match_score >= 75:
            ws_match_type = 'POTENTIAL'
            ws_case_status = 'REVIEW'
        case ws_match_score >= 50:
            ws_match_type = 'WEAK'
            ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'
        case _:
            ws_match_type = 'FALSE POSITIVE'
            ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'
    return ws_match_type, ws_sar_required, ws_case_status

def sixteen_thousand_two_hundred_C9(ws_customer_ssn: str, ws_customer_dob: str, ws_customer_name: str, id_request: str, id_response: str, addr_request: str, addr_response: str, ws_doc_type: str, ws_passport_number: str, ws_passport_country: str, passport_req: str, passport_resp: str, ws_license_number: str, ws_license_state: str, license_req: str, license_resp: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:

def sixteen_thousand_four_hundred_C9(ws_daily_trans_count: int, ws_velocity_threshold: int, ws_daily_trans_amount: Decimal, ws_amount_threshold: Decimal, ws_round_amount_count: int, ws_structuring_detected: str, ws_high_risk_country: str, ws_new_device: str) -> int:
    """16400-transaction_monitoring
    """
    ws_fraud_score = 0
    ws_velocity_flag, ws_amount_flag = sixteen_thousand_four_hundred_ten_C9(ws_daily_trans_count, ws_velocity_threshold, ws_daily_trans_amount, ws_amount_threshold, ws_fraud_score)
    ws_pattern_flag = sixteen_thousand_four_hundred_twenty_C9(ws_round_amount_count, ws_structuring_detected, ws_fraud_score)
    ws_location_flag, ws_device_flag = sixteen_thousand_four_hundred_thirty_C9(ws_high_risk_country, ws_new_device, ws_fraud_score)
    ws_fraud_decision, ws_manual_review = sixteen_thousand_four_hundred_forty_C9(ws_fraud_score)
    return ws_fraud_score, ws_velocity_flag, ws_amount_flag, ws_pattern_flag, ws_location_flag, ws_device_flag, ws_fraud_decision, ws_manual_review

def sixteen_thousand_four_hundred_ten_C9(ws_daily_trans_count: int, ws_velocity_threshold: int, ws_daily_trans_amount: Decimal, ws_amount_threshold: Decimal, ws_fraud_score: int) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """16410-check_velocity
    """
    ws_velocity_flag = 'N'
    ws_amount_flag = 'N'
    if ws_daily_trans_count > ws_velocity_threshold:
        ws_velocity_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 20
    if ws_daily_trans_amount > ws_amount_threshold:
        ws_amount_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 20
    return ws_velocity_flag, ws_amount_flag

def sixteen_thousand_four_hundred_twenty_C9(ws_round_amount_count: int, ws_structuring_detected: str, ws_fraud_score: int) -> str:
    """16420-check_patterns
    """
    ws_pattern_flag = 'N'
    if ws_round_amount_count > 5:
        ws_pattern_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 15
    if ws_structuring_detected == 'Y':
        ws_pattern_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 30
    return ws_pattern_flag

def sixteen_thousand_four_hundred_thirty_C9(ws_high_risk_country: str, ws_new_device: str, ws_fraud_score: int) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """16430-check_high_risk
    """
    ws_location_flag = 'N'
    ws_device_flag = 'N'
    if ws_high_risk_country == 'Y':
        ws_location_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 25
    if ws_new_device == 'Y':
        ws_device_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 10
    return ws_location_flag, ws_device_flag

def sixteen_thousand_four_hundred_forty_C9(ws_fraud_score: int) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """16440-calculate_risk_score
    """
    ws_manual_review = 'N'
    match True:
        case ws_fraud_score >= 80:
            ws_fraud_decision = 'BLOCK'
            ws_manual_review = 'Y'
        case ws_fraud_score >= 60:
            ws_fraud_decision = 'REVIEW'
            ws_manual_review = 'Y'
        case ws_fraud_score >= 40:
            ws_fraud_decision = 'MONITOR'
        case _:
            ws_fraud_decision = 'APPROVE'
    return ws_fraud_decision, ws_manual_review

        sar_record = sixteen_thousand_five_hundred_twenty_C9(sar_data)
        sar_record = sixteen_thousand_five_hundred_thirty_C9(sar_record)
    return sar_record

def seventeen_thousand_C9(ws_open_date: str, ws_case_type: str, ws_queue: str, ws_assigned_agent: str, ws_channel: str, ws_customer_account: str, history_file: str, ws_customer_id: str, case_file: str, ws_billing_error: str, ws_credit_amount: Decimal, ws_fraud_case: str, ws_follow_up_required: str, ws_customer_phone: str, case_record: str, card_request: str, reset_request: str, reset_resp: str, credit_record: str) -> None:
    """17000-customer_service
    """
    case_id = seventeen_thousand_one_hundred_C9(ws_open_date, ws_case_type)
    ws_queue, ws_assigned_agent = seventeen_thousand_two_hundred_C9(ws_case_type, ws_assigned_agent)
    ws_interaction_count = 0
    interaction = {}
    resolution_code, ws_fraud_case, card_request, reset_request, reset_resp, credit_record = seventeen_thousand_three_hundred_C9(ws_channel, ws_assigned_agent, ws_customer_account, history_file, ws_customer_id, case_file, ws_case_type, ws_billing_error, ws_credit_amount, ws_fraud_case, card_request, reset_request, reset_resp, credit_record)
    case_record = seventeen_thousand_four_hundred_C9(resolution_code, case_id, case_record)
    seventeen_thousand_five_hundred_C9(ws_follow_up_required, ws_customer_phone, case_id)
    return case_id, ws_queue, ws_assigned_agent, ws_interaction_count, interaction, resolution_code, ws_fraud_case, card_request, reset_request, reset_resp, credit_record, case_record

def seventeen_thousand_one_hundred_C9(ws_open_date: str, ws_case_type: str) -> str:

    ws_open_date = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")  # Format as YYYYMMDD
    ws_case_status = 'OPEN'
    ws_case_priority, ws_target_date = seventeen_thousand_one_hundred_twenty_C9(ws_case_type, ws_open_date)
    return ws_case_id

def seventeen_thousand_one_hundred_ten_C9() -> str:
    """17110-generate_case_id
    """
    ws_date_part = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")  # Format as YYYYMMDD
    ws_random_part = int(random.random() * 99999)
    ws_case_id = f'CS{ws_date_part}{ws_random_part}'
    return ws_case_id


def seventeen_thousand_one_hundred_twenty_C9(ws_case_type: str, ws_open_date: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """17120-categorize_case
    """
    match ws_case_type:
        case 'BILLING INQUIRY':
            ws_case_priority = 2
        case 'FRAUD REPORT':
            ws_case_priority = 1
        case 'ACCOUNT ACCESS':
            ws_case_priority = 1
        case 'GENERAL INQUIRY':
            ws_case_priority = 3
        case _:
            ws_case_priority = 3
    ws_target_date = int(datetime.strptime(ws_open_date, "%Y%m%d").strftime("%j")) + ws_case_priority * 2 #FUNCTION integer_of_date not same, using DOY
    return ws_case_priority, ws_target_date

def seventeen_thousand_three_hundred_thirty_C9(ws_case_type: str, ws_billing_error: str, ws_credit_amount: Decimal, ws_fraud_case: str, card_request: str, reset_request: str, reset_resp: str, credit_record: str) -> tuple[str, str, str, str, str, str]:
    """17330-determine_resolution
    """
    match ws_case_type:
        case 'BILLING INQUIRY':
            resolution_code, credit_record = seventeen_thousand_three_hundred_thirty_two_C9(ws_billing_error, ws_credit_amount, credit_record)
        case 'FRAUD REPORT':
            ws_fraud_case, card_request = seventeen_thousand_three_hundred_thirty_four_C9(card_request)
            resolution_code = 'FRAUD REMEDIATED'
        case 'ACCOUNT ACCESS':
            reset_request, reset_resp = seventeen_thousand_three_hundred_thirty_six_C9(reset_request, reset_resp)
            resolution_code = 'ACCESS RESTORED'
        case _:
            resolution_code = 'INFORMATION PROVIDED'
            card_request = ""
            reset_request = ""
            reset_resp = ""
            credit_record = ""
            ws_fraud_case = ""
    return resolution_code, ws_fraud_case, card_request, reset_request, reset_resp, credit_record

    def data_analytics_and_reporting_procedures_C10(self) -> None:
            """TODO"""
        self.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal("0.00") # MOVE ZEROES TO ws_total_trans_amount
        self.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO ws_total_trans_count
        self.WS_AVG_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal("0.00") # MOVE ZEROES TO ws_avg_trans_amount
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y': # PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            # READ transaction_file INTO ws_trans_rec
            try:
                transaction = self.TRANSACTION_FILE
                self.WS_TRANS_REC = transaction
                # NOT AT END
                self.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT += 1 # ADD 1 TO ws_total_trans_count
                self.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT += transaction.trans_amount # ADD trans_amount TO ws_total_trans_amount
            except EOFError:
                # AT END
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO ws_eof_flag
                break

        if self.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT > 0: # IF ws_total_trans_count > 0
            self.WS_AVG_TRANS_AMOUNT = self.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT / self.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT # COMPUTE ws_avg_trans_amount = ws_total_trans_amount / ws_total_trans_count
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO ws_eof_flag

    def collect_customer_metrics_C10(self) -> None:
            """TODO"""
        self.WS_DAILY_SUMMARY = WsDailySummary() # INITIALIZE ws_daily_summary
        self.DAILY_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE # MOVE ws_process_date TO daily_date
        self.DAILY_TRANS_COUNT = self.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT # MOVE ws_total_trans_count TO daily_trans_count
        self.DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT = self.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT # MOVE ws_total_trans_amount TO daily_trans_amount
        self.DAILY_DEPOSITS = Decimal("0.00")  # MOVE ws_total_deposits TO daily_deposits - Assuming ws_total_deposits
        self.DAILY_WITHDRAWALS = Decimal("0.00") # MOVE ws_total_withdrawals TO daily_withdrawals - Assuming ws_total_withdrawals
        self.DAILY_SUMMARY_RECORD = self.WS_DAILY_SUMMARY
        # WRITE daily_summary_record FROM ws_daily_summary
        # In Python, writing to a file is usually done explicitly
        # Example:
        # with open("daily_summary.txt", "w") as f:
        #     f.write(str(self.WS_DAILY_SUMMARY))

    def weekly_aggregation_C10(self) -> None:
        """21220-weekly_aggregation."""
        if self.WS_DAY_OF_WEEK == 7: # IF ws_day_of_week = 7
            self.WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY = WsWeeklySummary() # INITIALIZE ws_weekly_summary
            self.WEEKLY_WEEK = self.WS_WEEK_NUMBER # MOVE ws_week_number TO weekly_week
            self.sum_week_data_C10() # PERFORM 21225-sum_week_data
            self.WEEKLY_SUMMARY_RECORD = self.WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY
            # WRITE weekly_summary_record FROM ws_weekly_summary
            # In Python, writing to a file is usually done explicitly
            # Example:
            # with open("weekly_summary.txt", "w") as f:
            #     f.write(str(self.WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY))

    def sum_week_data_C10(self) -> None:
        """21225-sum_week_data."""
        self.WEEKLY_TRANS_COUNT = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO weekly_trans_count
        self.WEEKLY_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal("0.00") # MOVE ZEROES TO weekly_trans_amount
        for _ in range(7): # PERFORM 7 TIMES
            self.WEEKLY_TRANS_COUNT += self.DAILY_TRANS_COUNT # ADD daily_trans_count TO weekly_trans_count
            self.WEEKLY_TRANS_AMOUNT += Decimal(self.DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT) # ADD daily_trans_amount TO weekly_trans_amount

    def monthly_aggregation_C10(self) -> None:
        """21230-monthly_aggregation."""
        if self.WS_END_OF_MONTH == 'Y': # IF ws_end_of_month = 'Y'
            self.WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY = WsMonthlySummary() # INITIALIZE ws_monthly_summary
            self.MONTHLY_MONTH = self.WS_CURR_MONTH # MOVE ws_curr_month TO monthly_month
            self.MONTHLY_YEAR = self.WS_CURR_YEAR # MOVE ws_curr_year TO monthly_year
            self.sum_month_data_C10() # PERFORM 21235-sum_month_data
            self.MONTHLY_SUMMARY_RECORD = self.WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY
            # WRITE monthly_summary_record FROM ws_monthly_summary
            # In Python, writing to a file is usually done explicitly
            # Example:
            # with open("monthly_summary.txt", "w") as f:
            #     f.write(str(self.WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY))

    def sum_month_data_C10(self) -> None:
        """21235-sum_month_data."""
        self.MONTHLY_TRANS_COUNT = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO monthly_trans_count
        self.MONTHLY_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal("0.00") # MOVE ZEROES TO monthly_trans_amount
        self.MONTHLY_NEW_ACCOUNTS = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO monthly_new_accounts
        self.MONTHLY_CLOSED_ACCOUNTS = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO monthly_closed_accounts
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y': # PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            # READ daily_summary_file INTO ws_daily_sum_rec
            try:
                daily_summary = self.DAILY_SUMMARY_FILE
                self.WS_DAILY_SUM_REC = daily_summary
                # NOT AT END
                if daily_summary.daily_month == self.WS_CURR_MONTH: # IF daily_month = ws_curr_month
                    self.MONTHLY_TRANS_COUNT += daily_summary.daily_trans_count # ADD daily_trans_count TO monthly_trans_count
                    self.MONTHLY_TRANS_AMOUNT += daily_summary.daily_trans_amount # ADD daily_trans_amount TO monthly_trans_amount
            except EOFError:
                # AT END
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO ws_eof_flag
                break
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO ws_eof_flag

    def calculate_kpi_C10(self) -> None:
            """TODO"""

    ship_est_delivery: int = 0

def anonymous_C11() -> None:
        """TODO"""
    global WS_WIRE_VALID, WS_WIRE_REJECT, WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT, WS_CTR_REQUIRED
    WS_WIRE_VALID = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO ws_wire_valid
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT <= 0:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO ws_wire_valid
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INVALID AMOUNT'  # MOVE 'INVALID AMOUNT' TO ws_wire_reject
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO ws_wire_valid
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'  # MOVE 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS' TO ws_wire_reject
    if WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT == " " * len(WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT):
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO ws_wire_valid
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED'  # MOVE 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED' TO ws_wire_reject
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > 10000:
        WS_CTR_REQUIRED = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO ws_ctr_required

def cvvverify(card_number: str, cvv: str) -> str:
    """Dummy implementation for CVVVERIFY"""
    return 'M' if cvv == '123' else 'N'

def fraudcheck(auth_request: str) -> dict:
    """Dummy implementation for FRAUDCHECK"""
    return {"fraud_score": 50, "fraud_decline_code": "05"}

def read_card_account_file(search_key: str) -> dict:
    """Dummy implementation for card_account_file read"""
    if search_key == "1234567890123456":
        return {"available_credit": Decimal("1000.00")}
    else:
        return {"available_credit": Decimal("0.00")}
