def validate_stop_request_C12(ws_check_number: int, ws_check_already_cleared: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """COBOL: 29100-validate_stop_request."""
    ws_stop_valid: str = 'Y'  # 29100
    ws_stop_reject: str = ''

    if ws_check_number == 0: # 29100
        ws_stop_valid = 'N' # 29100
        ws_stop_reject = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED' # 29100

    if ws_check_already_cleared == 'Y': # 29100
        ws_stop_valid = 'N' # 29100
        ws_stop_reject = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED' # 29100

    return ws_stop_valid, ws_stop_reject

def create_stop_order_C12(acct_id: str, ws_check_number: str, ws_check_amount: Decimal, ws_payee_name: str, ws_process_date: str) -> None:
    """COBOL: 29200-create_stop_order."""
    ws_stop_record: StopRecord = StopRecord() # 29200
    stop_record: StopRecord

    ws_stop_record.stop_account = acct_id  # 29200
    ws_stop_record.stop_check_number = ws_check_number  # 29200
    ws_stop_record.stop_amount = ws_check_amount  # 29200
    ws_stop_record.stop_payee = ws_payee_name  # 29200
    ws_stop_record.stop_effective_date = ws_process_date  # 29200
    ws_stop_record.stop_expiry_date = int(ws_process_date.replace("-", "")) + 180  # 29200
    ws_stop_record.stop_status = 'A'  # 29200

    write_stop_record(ws_stop_record)  # 29200

def apply_stop_fee_C12(ws_stop_payment_fee: Decimal, ws_account_balance: Decimal, ws_check_number: str, update_account_C12: callable, send_notification_C12: callable) -> Decimal:
    """COBOL: 29300-apply_stop_fee."""
    ws_account_balance -= ws_stop_payment_fee  # 29300
    update_account_C12(ws_account_balance)  # 29300
    ws_notif_type: str = 'stop_payment'  # 29300
    ws_notif_channel: str = 'EMAIL'  # 29300
    ws_notif_subject: str = f'Stop payment placed on check #{ws_check_number}'  # 29300
    send_notification_C12() # 29300
    return ws_account_balance


def safe_deposit_box_C12(box_rental_C12: callable, box_access_C12: callable, box_drilling_C12: callable, box_billing_C12: callable) -> None:
    """COBOL: 30000-safe_deposit_box."""
    box_rental_C12()  # 30000
    box_access_C12()  # 30000
    box_drilling_C12()  # 30000
    box_billing_C12()  # 30000

def box_rental_C12(ws_rental_request: str, check_availability_C12: callable, assign_box_C12: callable, create_rental_agreement_C12: callable) -> None:
    """COBOL: 30100-box_rental."""
    if ws_rental_request == 'Y':  # 30100
        check_availability_C12()  # 30100
        if ws_box_available == 'Y':  # 30100
            assign_box_C12()  # 30100
            create_rental_agreement_C12()  # 30100

def check_availability_C12(ws_total_boxes: int, ws_requested_size: str, box_status: list, box_size: list) -> None:
    """COBOL: 30110-check_availability."""
    global ws_box_available, ws_assigned_box #30110
    ws_box_available = 'N'  # 30110

    for ws_box_idx in range(1, ws_total_boxes + 1):  # 30110
        if box_status[ws_box_idx - 1] == 'A':  # 30110
            if box_size[ws_box_idx - 1] == ws_requested_size:  # 30110
                ws_box_available = 'Y'  # 30110
                ws_assigned_box = ws_box_idx  # 30110
                break  # 30110

def assign_box_C12(ws_assigned_box: int, ws_customer_id: str, ws_process_date: str, box_status: list, box_renter: list, box_rental_date: list) -> None:
    """COBOL: 30120-assign_box."""
    box_status[ws_assigned_box - 1] = 'R'  # 30120
    box_renter[ws_assigned_box - 1] = ws_customer_id  # 30120
    box_rental_date[ws_assigned_box - 1] = ws_process_date  # 30120

def create_rental_agreement_C12(ws_assigned_box: int, ws_customer_id: str, ws_process_date: str, ws_box_size_fee: list, ws_requested_size: str) -> None:
    """COBOL: 30130-create_rental_agreement."""
    ws_rental_agreement: RentalAgreement = RentalAgreement()  # 30130

    ws_rental_agreement.rental_box_number = str(ws_assigned_box)  # 30130
    ws_rental_agreement.rental_customer = ws_customer_id  # 30130
    ws_rental_agreement.rental_start_date = ws_process_date  # 30130
    ws_rental_agreement.rental_annual_fee = ws_box_size_fee[int(ws_requested_size)]  # 30130

    write_rental_record(ws_rental_agreement)  # 30130

def box_access_C12(ws_access_request: str, verify_renter_C12: callable, log_access_C12: callable, escort_to_vault_C12: callable) -> None:
    """COBOL: 30200-box_access."""
    if ws_access_request == 'Y':  # 30200
        verify_renter_C12()  # 30200
        if ws_renter_verified == 'Y':  # 30200
            log_access_C12()  # 30200
            escort_to_vault_C12()  # 30200

def verify_renter_C12(ws_box_number: int, ws_customer_id: str, ws_id_verified: str, ws_key_verified: str, box_renter: list) -> None:
    """COBOL: 30210-verify_renter."""
    global ws_renter_verified #30210
    ws_renter_verified = 'N'  # 30210
    if box_renter[ws_box_number - 1] == ws_customer_id:  # 30210
        if ws_id_verified == 'Y':  # 30210
            if ws_key_verified == 'Y':  # 30210
                ws_renter_verified = 'Y'  # 30210

def log_access_C12(ws_box_number: int, ws_customer_id: str, ws_process_date: str) -> None:
    """COBOL: 30220-log_access."""
    ws_access_log: AccessLog = AccessLog()  # 30220

    ws_access_log.access_box_number = str(ws_box_number)  # 30220
    ws_access_log.access_customer = ws_customer_id  # 30220
    ws_access_log.access_date = ws_process_date  # 30220
    ws_access_log.access_time = datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S")  # 30220
    ws_access_log.access_type = 'ENTRY'  # 30220

    write_access_log_record(ws_access_log)  # 30220

def escort_to_vault_C12() -> None:
    """COBOL: 30230-escort_to_vault."""
    ws_display_msg: str = 'VAULT ACCESS GRANTED'  # 30230
    print(ws_display_msg)  # 30230

def box_drilling_C12(ws_drilling_request: str, validate_drilling_auth_C12: callable, schedule_drilling_C12: callable, notify_renter_C12: callable) -> None:
    """COBOL: 30300-box_drilling."""
    if ws_drilling_request == 'Y':  # 30300
        validate_drilling_auth_C12()  # 30300
        if ws_drilling_authorized == 'Y':  # 30300
            schedule_drilling_C12()  # 30300
            notify_renter_C12()  # 30300

def validate_drilling_auth_C12(ws_rent_delinquent_months: int, ws_court_order: str, ws_deceased_renter: str, ws_executor_verified: str) -> None:
    """COBOL: 30310-validate_drilling_auth."""
    global ws_drilling_authorized #30310
    ws_drilling_authorized = 'N'  # 30310

    if ws_rent_delinquent_months >= 12:  # 30310
        ws_drilling_authorized = 'Y'  # 30310

    if ws_court_order == 'Y':  # 30310
        ws_drilling_authorized = 'Y'  # 30310

    if ws_deceased_renter == 'Y':  # 30310
        if ws_executor_verified == 'Y':  # 30310
            ws_drilling_authorized = 'Y'  # 30310

def schedule_drilling_C12(ws_box_number: int, ws_drilling_reason: str, ws_process_date: str) -> None:
    """COBOL: 30320-schedule_drilling."""
    ws_drilling_record: DrillingRecord = DrillingRecord()  # 30320

    ws_drilling_record.drill_box_number = str(ws_box_number)  # 30320
    ws_drilling_record.drill_reason = ws_drilling_reason  # 30320
    ws_drilling_record.drill_scheduled_date = int(ws_process_date.replace("-","")) + 30  # 30320

    write_drilling_record(ws_drilling_record)  # 30320

def notify_renter_C12(send_notification_C12: callable) -> None:
    """COBOL: 30330-notify_renter."""
    ws_notif_type: str = 'box_drilling'  # 30330
    ws_notif_channel: str = 'MAIL'  # 30330
    ws_notif_subject: str = 'Important notice regarding your safe deposit box'  # 30330
    send_notification_C12()  # 30330

def box_billing_C12(ws_total_boxes: int, box_status: list, box_renewal_due: list, charge_annual_fee_C12: callable) -> None:
    """COBOL: 30400-box_billing."""
    for ws_box_idx in range(1, ws_total_boxes + 1):  # 30400
        if box_status[ws_box_idx - 1] == 'R':  # 30400
            if box_renewal_due[ws_box_idx - 1] == 'Y':  # 30400
                charge_annual_fee_C12(ws_box_idx)  # 30400

def charge_annual_fee_C12(ws_box_idx: int, ws_account_balance: Decimal, box_renter: list, box_annual_fee: list, box_next_renewal: list, update_account_C12: callable) -> Decimal:
    """COBOL: 30410-charge_annual_fee."""
    ws_customer_id: str = box_renter[ws_box_idx - 1]  # 30410
    ws_fee_amount: Decimal = box_annual_fee[ws_box_idx - 1]  # 30410
    ws_account_balance -= ws_fee_amount  # 30410
    update_account_C12(ws_account_balance)  # 30410
    box_next_renewal[ws_box_idx - 1] += 10000  # 30410
    return ws_account_balance

def merchant_services_C12(process_authorization_C12: callable, capture_transaction_C12: callable, process_settlement_C12: callable, handle_chargeback_C12: callable) -> None:
    """COBOL: 31000-merchant_services."""
    process_authorization_C12()  # 31000
    capture_transaction_C12()  # 31000
    process_settlement_C12()  # 31000
    handle_chargeback_C12()  # 31000

def process_authorization_C12(validate_card_C12: callable, check_fraud_score_C12: callable, check_available_credit_C12: callable, approve_auth_C12: callable, decline_auth_C12: callable) -> None:
    """COBOL: 31100-process_authorization."""
    validate_card_C12()  # 31100
    if ws_card_valid == 'Y':  # 31100
        check_fraud_score_C12()  # 31100
        if ws_fraud_approved == 'Y':  # 31100
            check_available_credit_C12()  # 31100
            if ws_credit_available == 'Y':  # 31100
                approve_auth_C12()  # 31100
            else:  # 31100
                decline_auth_C12()  # 31100
        else:  # 31100
            decline_auth_C12()  # 31100
    else:  # 31100
        decline_auth_C12()  # 31100

def validate_card_C12(check_luhn_C12: callable, check_expiry_C12: callable, check_cvv_C12: callable) -> None:
    """COBOL: 31110-validate_card."""
    global ws_card_valid #31110
    ws_card_valid = 'N'  # 31110
    check_luhn_C12()  # 31110
    if ws_luhn_valid == 'Y':  # 31110
        check_expiry_C12()  # 31110
        if ws_not_expired == 'Y':  # 31110
            check_cvv_C12()  # 31110
            if ws_cvv_valid == 'Y':  # 31110
                ws_card_valid = 'Y'  # 31110

def check_luhn_C12(ws_auth_card_number: str) -> None:
    """COBOL: 31115-check_luhn."""
    global ws_luhn_valid #31115
    ws_luhn_sum: int = 0  # 31115

    for ws_luhn_idx in range(16, 0, -1):  # 31115
        ws_luhn_digit: int = int(ws_auth_card_number[ws_luhn_idx - 1])  # 31115
        if (17 - ws_luhn_idx) % 2 == 0:  # 31115
            ws_luhn_digit *= 2  # 31115
            if ws_luhn_digit > 9:  # 31115
                ws_luhn_digit -= 9  # 31115
        ws_luhn_sum += ws_luhn_digit  # 31115

    if ws_luhn_sum % 10 == 0:  # 31115
        ws_luhn_valid = 'Y'  # 31115
    else:  # 31115
        ws_luhn_valid = 'N'  # 31115

def check_expiry_C12(ws_auth_expiry_date: str, ws_process_date: str) -> None:
    """COBOL: 31116-check_expiry."""
    global ws_not_expired #31116
    if ws_auth_expiry_date >= ws_process_date:  # 31116
        ws_not_expired = 'Y'  # 31116
    else:  # 31116
        ws_not_expired = 'N'  # 31116

def check_cvv_C12(ws_auth_card_number: str, ws_auth_cvv: str, cvvverify_func: callable = cvvverify) -> None:
    """COBOL: 31117-check_cvv."""
    global ws_cvv_valid #31117
    ws_cvv_result: str = cvvverify_func(ws_auth_card_number, ws_auth_cvv)  # 31117
    if ws_cvv_result == 'M':  # 31117
        ws_cvv_valid = 'Y'  # 31117
    else:  # 31117
        ws_cvv_valid = 'N'  # 31117

def check_fraud_score_C12(ws_auth_request: str, fraudcheck_func: callable = fraudcheck) -> None:
    """COBOL: 31120-check_fraud_score."""
    global ws_fraud_approved, ws_auth_decline_code, FRAUD_SCORE #31120
    fraud_response: dict = fraudcheck_func(ws_auth_request)  # 31120
    fraud_score: int = fraud_response["fraud_score"]
    fraud_decline_code: str = fraud_response["fraud_decline_code"]

    if fraud_score < 70:  # 31120
        ws_fraud_approved = 'Y'  # 31120
    else:  # 31120
        ws_fraud_approved = 'N'  # 31120
        ws_auth_decline_code = fraud_decline_code  # 31120

def check_available_credit_C12(ws_auth_amount: Decimal, ws_auth_card_number: str, read_card_account_file_func: callable = read_card_account_file) -> None:
    """COBOL: 31130-check_available_credit."""
    global ws_credit_available, ws_auth_decline_code, WS_CARD_ACCOUNT_REC, WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT, WS_SEARCH_KEY #31130
    WS_SEARCH_KEY = ws_auth_card_number  # 31130
    WS_CARD_ACCOUNT_REC = read_card_account_file_func(WS_SEARCH_KEY)  # 31130
    WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT = WS_CARD_ACCOUNT_REC["available_credit"]
    if WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT >= ws_auth_amount:  # 31130
        ws_credit_available = 'Y'  # 31130
    else:  # 31130
        ws_credit_available = 'N'  # 31130
        ws_auth_decline_code = '51'  # 31130

def approve_auth_C12(ws_auth_amount: Decimal, generate_auth_code_C12: callable, record_authorization_C12: callable) -> None:
    """COBOL: 31140-approve_auth."""
    global ws_auth_response_code, WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT#31140
    ws_auth_response_code = '00'  # 31140
    generate_auth_code_C12()  # 31140
    WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT -= ws_auth_amount  # 31140
    record_authorization_C12()  # 31140

def generate_auth_code_C12() -> None:
    """COBOL: 31145-generate_auth_code."""
    import random
    global ws_auth_code, ws_auth_response_auth_code #31145
    ws_auth_code = random.random() * 999999  # 31145
    ws_auth_response_auth_code = str(int(ws_auth_code))  # 31145

def record_authorization_C12(ws_auth_card_number: str, ws_auth_amount: Decimal, ws_auth_response_auth_code: str, ws_process_date: str, ws_merchant_id: str) -> None:
    """COBOL: 31146-record_authorization."""
    ws_auth_record: AuthRecord = AuthRecord()  # 31146

    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_card = ws_auth_card_number  # 31146
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_amount = ws_auth_amount  # 31146
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_code = ws_auth_response_auth_code  # 31146
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_date = ws_process_date  # 31146
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_time = datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S")  # 31146
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_merchant = ws_merchant_id  # 31146
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_status = 'P'  # 31146

    write_auth_record(ws_auth_record)  # 31146

def decline_auth_C12(ws_auth_card_number: str, ws_auth_amount: Decimal, ws_auth_decline_code: str, ws_process_date: str) -> None:
    """COBOL: 31150-decline_auth."""
    global ws_auth_response_code#31150
    ws_auth_response_code = ws_auth_decline_code  # 31150

    ws_decline_record: DeclineRecord = DeclineRecord()  # 31150

    ws_decline_record.decline_rec_card = ws_auth_card_number  # 31150
    ws_decline_record.decline_rec_amount = ws_auth_amount  # 31150
    ws_decline_record.decline_rec_code = ws_auth_decline_code  #

def logging_utilities_C13() -> None:
    """99800-logging_utilities."""
    log_info_C13()
    log_warning_C13()
    log_error_C13()

def log_info_C13() -> None:
    """99810-log_info."""
    global LOG_LEVEL, WS_LOG_MESSAGE, LOG_TIMESTAMP, WS_LOG_ENTRY
    LOG_LEVEL = 'INFO'  # MOVE 'INFO' TO log_level
    WS_LOG_MESSAGE = WS_LOG_MESSAGE  # MOVE ws_log_message TO log_message
    LOG_TIMESTAMP = str(datetime.now())  # MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO log_timestamp
    # WRITE log_record FROM ws_log_entry
    print(f"Writing to log: {WS_LOG_ENTRY}")

def log_warning_C13() -> None:
    """99820-log_warning."""
    global LOG_LEVEL, WS_LOG_MESSAGE, LOG_TIMESTAMP, WS_LOG_ENTRY
    LOG_LEVEL = 'WARN'  # MOVE 'WARN' TO log_level
    WS_LOG_MESSAGE = WS_LOG_MESSAGE  # MOVE ws_log_message TO log_message
    LOG_TIMESTAMP = str(datetime.now())  # MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO log_timestamp
    # WRITE log_record FROM ws_log_entry
    print(f"Writing to log: {WS_LOG_ENTRY}")

def log_error_C13() -> None:
    """99830-log_error."""
    global LOG_LEVEL, WS_LOG_MESSAGE, LOG_TIMESTAMP, WS_LOG_ENTRY
    LOG_LEVEL = 'ERROR'  # MOVE 'ERROR' TO log_level
    WS_LOG_MESSAGE = WS_LOG_MESSAGE  # MOVE ws_log_message TO log_message
    LOG_TIMESTAMP = str(datetime.now())  # MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO log_timestamp
    # WRITE log_record FROM ws_log_entry
    print(f"Writing to log: {WS_LOG_ENTRY}")

def error_handling_C13() -> None:
    """99900-error_handling."""
    format_error_C13()
    display_error_C13()
    write_error_log_C13()

def format_error_C13() -> None:
    """99910-format_error."""
    global WS_FORMATTED_ERROR, WS_ERROR_CODE, WS_ERROR_MSG
    WS_FORMATTED_ERROR = f"ERROR: {WS_ERROR_CODE} - {WS_ERROR_MSG}" # STRING 'ERROR: ' DELIMITED SIZE ws_error_code DELIMITED SIZE ' - ' DELIMITED SIZE ws_error_msg DELIMITED SIZE INTO ws_formatted_error
def display_error_C13() -> None:
    """99920-display_error."""
    global WS_FORMATTED_ERROR
    print(WS_FORMATTED_ERROR)  # DISPLAY ws_formatted_error
def write_error_log_C13() -> None:
    """99930-write_error_log."""
    global WS_ERROR_LOG_REC, ERR_LOG_CODE, ERR_LOG_MSG, ERR_LOG_TIMESTAMP, WS_PROGRAM_NAME, WS_PARAGRAPH_NAME
    WS_ERROR_LOG_REC = ""  # INITIALIZE ws_error_log_rec
    ERR_LOG_CODE = WS_ERROR_CODE  # MOVE ws_error_code TO err_log_code
    ERR_LOG_MSG = WS_ERROR_MSG  # MOVE ws_error_msg TO err_log_msg
    ERR_LOG_TIMESTAMP = str(datetime.now())  # MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO err_log_timestamp
    WS_PROGRAM_NAME = WS_PROGRAM_NAME  # MOVE ws_program_name TO err_log_program
    WS_PARAGRAPH_NAME = WS_PARAGRAPH_NAME  # MOVE ws_paragraph_name TO err_log_paragraph
    # WRITE error_log_record FROM ws_error_log_rec
    print(f"Writing error log record: {WS_ERROR_LOG_REC}")

def treasury_management_C13() -> None:
    """32000-treasury_management."""
    calculate_cash_position_C13()
    project_cash_flows_C13()
    manage

def _34110_calculate_tier1_C14() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_TIER1_CAPITAL, WS_COMMON_STOCK, WS_RETAINED_EARNINGS, WS_AOCI, WS_GOODWILL, WS_INTANGIBLES, WS_DTA_DEDUCTION
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += None  # TODO: was WS_COMMON_STOCK
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += WS_RETAINED_EARNINGS
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += None  # TODO: was WS_AOCI
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= None  # TODO: was WS_GOODWILL
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= None  # TODO: was WS_INTANGIBLES
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= None  # TODO: was WS_DTA_DEDUCTION

def _34120_calculate_tier2_C14() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_TIER2_CAPITAL, WS_SUB_DEBT, WS_ALLL_ELIGIBLE, WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL, WS_TIER1_CAPITAL
    WS_TIER2_CAPITAL = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TIER2_CAPITAL += None  # TODO: was WS_SUB_DEBT
    WS_TIER2_CAPITAL += None  # TODO: was WS_ALLL_ELIGIBLE
    WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL = WS_TIER1_CAPITAL + WS_TIER2_CAPITAL

def _34130_calculate_ratios_C14() -> None:
    """34130-calculate_ratios
    IF ws_risk_weighted_assets > 0
       COMPUTE ws_cet1_ratio = 
          (ws_tier1_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
       COMPUTE ws_capital_ratio = 
          (ws_total_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
    
    IF ws_total_assets > 0
       COMPUTE ws_leverage_ratio = 
          (ws_tier1_capital / ws_total_assets) * 100
    
    global WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS, WS_TIER1_CAPITAL, WS_CET1_RATIO, WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL, WS_CAPITAL_RATIO, WS_TOTAL_ASSETS, WS_LEVERAGE_RATIO
    if WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS > Decimal("0.00"):
        WS_CET1_RATIO = (WS_TIER1_CAPITAL / WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS) * Decimal("100.00")
        WS_CAPITAL_RATIO = (WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL / WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS) * Decimal("100.00")
    if WS_TOTAL_ASSETS > Decimal("0.00"):
        WS_LEVERAGE_RATIO = (WS_TIER1_CAPITAL / WS_TOTAL_ASSETS) * Decimal("100.00")

def _34200_risk_weighted_assets_C14() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS = Decimal("0.00")
    _34210_credit_rwa_C14()
    _34220_market_rwa_C14()
    _34230_operational_rwa_C14()

def _34210_credit_rwa_C14() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_CASH_POSITION, WS_GOVT_SECURITIES, WS_BANK_DEPOSITS, WS_RESIDENTIAL_MORTGAGES, WS_COMMERCIAL_LOANS, WS_CONSUMER_LOANS
    global WS_CASH_RWA, WS_GOVT_RWA, WS_BANK_RWA, WS_MORTGAGE_RWA, WS_COMMERCIAL_RWA, WS_CONSUMER_RWA, WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS

    WS_CASH_RWA = WS_CASH_POSITION * Decimal("0.00")
    WS_GOVT_RWA = WS_GOVT_SECURITIES * Decimal("0.00")
    WS_BANK_RWA = WS_BANK_DEPOSITS * Decimal("0.20")
    WS_MORTGAGE_RWA = WS_RESIDENTIAL_MORTGAGES * Decimal("0.50")
    WS_COMMERCIAL_RWA = WS_COMMERCIAL_LOANS * Decimal("1.00")
    WS_CONSUMER_RWA = WS_CONSUMER_LOANS * Decimal("1.00")

    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += None  # TODO: was WS_CASH_RWA
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += None  # TODO: was WS_GOVT_RWA
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += None  # TODO: was WS_BANK_RWA
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += None  # TODO: was WS_MORTGAGE_RWA
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += None  # TODO: was WS_COMMERCIAL_RWA
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += None  # TODO: was WS_CONSUMER_RWA

def _34220_market_rwa_C14() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_TRADING_ASSETS, WS_MARKET_RISK_FACTOR, WS_MARKET_RWA, WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS
    WS_MARKET_RWA = WS_TRADING_ASSETS * WS_MARKET_RISK_FACTOR
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += None  # TODO: was WS_MARKET_RWA

def _34230_operational_rwa_C14() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_GROSS_INCOME, WS_OPERATIONAL_FACTOR, WS_OPERATIONAL_RWA, WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS
    WS_OPERATIONAL_RWA = WS_GROSS_INCOME * WS_OPERATIONAL_FACTOR * Decimal("12.5")
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += None  # TODO: was WS_OPERATIONAL_RWA

def _34310_project_capital_needs_C14() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS, WS_GROWTH_RATE, WS_PROJECTED_RWA, WS_REQUIRED_CAPITAL, WS_TARGET_RATIO, WS_CAPITAL_GAP, WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL
    WS_PROJECTED_RWA = WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS * (Decimal("1.00") + WS_GROWTH_RATE)
    WS_REQUIRED_CAPITAL = WS_PROJECTED_RWA * WS_TARGET_RATIO / Decimal("100.00")
    WS_CAPITAL_GAP = WS_REQUIRED_CAPITAL - WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL

def _34320_identify_capital_actions_C14() -> None:
    """34320-identify_capital_actions
    IF ws_capital_gap > 0
       EVALUATE TRUE
          WHEN ws_capital_gap <= ws_retained_earnings_proj
             MOVE 'ORGANIC GROWTH' TO ws_capital_action
          WHEN ws_capital_gap <= ws_sub_debt_capacity
             MOVE 'SUB DEBT ISSUANCE' TO ws_capital_action
          WHEN OTHER
             MOVE 'EQUITY RAISE' TO ws_capital_action

       MOVE 'NO ACTION NEEDED' TO ws_capital_action
    
    global WS_CAPITAL_GAP, WS_RETAINED_EARNINGS_PROJ, WS_SUB_DEBT_CAPACITY, WS_CAPITAL_ACTION
    if WS_CAPITAL_GAP > Decimal("0.00"):
        if WS_CAPITAL_GAP <= WS_RETAINED_EARNINGS_PROJ:
            WS_CAPITAL_ACTION = 'ORGANIC GROWTH'
        elif WS_CAPITAL_GAP <= WS_SUB_DEBT_CAPACITY:
            WS_CAPITAL_ACTION = 'SUB DEBT ISSUANCE'
        else:
            WS_CAPITAL_ACTION = 'EQUITY RAISE'
    else:
        WS_CAPITAL_ACTION = 'NO ACTION NEEDED'

WS_GL_CONTROL_BAL = 0  # Assuming initial value

def perform_if_recon_diff_not_zeroes() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_RECON_DIFF, WS_GL_CONTROL_BAL, WS_SUBLEDGER_TOTAL
    WS_RECON_DIFF = WS_GL_CONTROL_BAL - WS_SUBLEDGER_TOTAL
    if WS_RECON_DIFF != 0:
        log_recon_exception_C15()

def intercompany_recon_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    load_ic_balances_C15()

def load_ic_balances_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_IC_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_IC_ARRAY, INTERCOMPANY_FILE, WS_IC_BALANCE_INST
    WS_IC_COUNT = 0
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        ic_balance = read_intercompany_file(INTERCOMPANY_FILE)
        if ic_balance is None:  # Simulate AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            WS_IC_COUNT += 1
            # Assign the read values to WS_IC_ARRAY element
            WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_COUNT - 1] = ic_balance
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def read_intercompany_file(filename: str) -> WS_IC_BALANCE | None:
  #Simulate reading from a file
  print("Reading IC file")
  return None

def match_ic_pairs_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_IC_COUNT, WS_IC_IDX
    WS_IC_IDX = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX <= WS_IC_COUNT:
        find_ic_counterpart_C15()
        WS_IC_IDX += 1


def find_ic_counterpart_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_IC_IDX, WS_IC_IDX2, WS_IC_COUNT, WS_SEARCH_FROM, WS_SEARCH_TO, WS_IC_DIFF, WS_IC_ARRAY
    WS_SEARCH_FROM = WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX - 1].IC_FROM_ENTITY
    WS_SEARCH_TO = WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX - 1].IC_TO_ENTITY
    WS_IC_IDX2 = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX2 <= WS_IC_COUNT:
        if WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].IC_FROM_ENTITY == WS_SEARCH_TO:
            if WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].IC_TO_ENTITY == WS_SEARCH_FROM:
                WS_IC_DIFF = WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX - 1].IC_AMOUNT + WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].IC_AMOUNT
                if WS_IC_DIFF != 0:
                    log_ic_diff_C15()
                break  # EXIT PERFORM
        WS_IC_IDX2 += 1


def log_ic_diff_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_IC_DIFF_REC_INST, WS_SEARCH_FROM, WS_SEARCH_TO, WS_IC_DIFF, IC_DIFF_RECORD
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC_INST = WS_IC_DIFF_REC()
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC_INST.ICD_FROM  = None  # TODO: was WS_SEARCH_FROM
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC_INST.ICD_TO  = None  # TODO: was WS_SEARCH_TO
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC_INST.ICD_AMOUNT = Decimal(str(WS_IC_DIFF))
    write_ic_diff_record(WS_IC_DIFF_REC_INST, IC_DIFF_RECORD)

def load_nostro_statement_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_NOSTRO_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_NOSTRO_ITEM, NOSTRO_STATEMENT_FILE, WS_NOSTRO_ITEM_INST
    WS_NOSTRO_COUNT = 0
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        nostro_item = read_nostro_statement_file(NOSTRO_STATEMENT_FILE)
        if nostro_item is None:  # Simulate AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            WS_NOSTRO_COUNT += 1
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def archive_audit_logs_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_END_OF_MONTH
    if WS_END_OF_MONTH == 'Y':
        move_to_archive_C15()
        compress_archive_C15()


WS_ARCHIVE_DATE = "20240101 00:00:00"
AUDIT_FILE = "audit.txt"

def move_to_archive_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_AUDIT_RECORD_INST, WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP, WS_ARCHIVE_DATE, AUDIT_FILE, ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORD
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        WS_AUDIT_RECORD_INST = read_audit_file(AUDIT_FILE)
        if WS_AUDIT_RECORD_INST is None:  # Simulate AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            if WS_AUDIT_RECORD_INST.WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP < WS_ARCHIVE_DATE:
                write_archive_audit_record(WS_AUDIT_RECORD_INST, ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORD)
                delete_audit_file(AUDIT_FILE)
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def cpu_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_CPU_UTILIZATION, WS_CPU_ALERT
    WS_CPU_UTILIZATION = getcpu()
    if WS_CPU_UTILIZATION > 80:
        WS_CPU_ALERT = 'Y'

    # Simulate external call
    print("GETCPU called")
